!!   - Heat sources/sinks with no terrain model yet: water, snow, fire, extreme temperatures; inferno missiles (inferno bins aren't fired from; they can still explode)
!!   - Heat decisions (bot logic, later): cooldown (a mech at max_heat stays there), voluntary shutdown; equipment uses Daniel's defaults (stealth always on, 1-on/1-off radical/MASC/supercharger, coolant pod when above min_heat, aimed shots at config.AIMED_SHOT_LOCATION)
!!   - Torso-mounted cockpit PSR rules
!!   - Physical attacks: all seven types are built; not priced in movement scoring: the PSRs a hit forces (only Movement-phase PSRs count), picking up a club as part of the plan; domino effects (no third mech)
!!   - Tripod leg sub-roll uses Python's random module (seeded from SEED, not the BattleRNG stream)
